Set<SeedRun>();\n\n    protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder model)\n    {\n        // Snake_case table and column names are not a style choice: the provided seed.sql\n        // targets these exact names, and executing it verbatim is what lets us treat the\n        // dataset as production data instead of rewriting it.\n        model.Entity<Account>(e =>\n        {\n            e.ToTable(\"accounts\");\n            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);\n            e.Property(x => x.Id).HasColumnName(\"id\").ValueGeneratedNever();\n            e.Property(x => x.Name).HasColumnName(\"name\").HasMaxLength(120).IsRequired();\n            e.Property(x => x.Industry).HasColumnName(\"industry\").HasMaxLength(60).IsRequired();\n            e.Property(x => x.Timezone).HasColumnName(\"timezone\").HasMaxLength(60).IsRequired();\n            e.Property(x => x.CreatedAt).HasColumnName(\"created_at\").HasColumnType(\"datetime2(0)\");\n        });\n\n        model.Entity<ActivityEvent>(e =>\n        {\n            e.ToTable(\"activity_events\");\n            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);\n            e.Property(x => x.Id).HasColumnName(\"id\").ValueGeneratedNever();\n            e.Property(x => x.AccountId).HasColumnName(\"account_id\");\n            e.Property(x => x.Location).HasColumnName(\"location\").HasMaxLength(80).IsRequired();\n            e.Property(x => x.EventType).HasColumnName(\"event_type\").HasMaxLength(40).IsRequired();\n            e.Property(x => x.OccurredAt).HasColumnName(\"occurred_at\").HasColumnType(\"datetime2(0)\");\n            e.Property(x => x.DurationSeconds).HasColumnName(\"duration_seconds\");\n            e.Property(x => x.Outcome).HasColumnName(\"outcome\").HasMaxLength(40);\n            e.Property(x => x.OccurredLocalDate).HasColumnName(\"occurred_local_date\").HasColumnType(\"date\");\n            e.Property(x => x.LocalWeekStart).HasColumnName(\"local_week_start\").HasColumnType(\"date\");\n\n            e.HasOne(x => x.Account)\n             .WithMany(a => a.Events)\n             .HasForeignKey(x => x.AccountId)\n             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);\n\n            // The pulse query filters on account + local week and groups by location.\n            e.HasIndex(x => new { x.AccountId, x.LocalWeekStart })\n             .HasDatabaseName(\"IX_activity_events_account_local_week\")\n             .IncludeProperties(x => new { x.Location, x.EventType });\n\n            // Deduplication groups on the whole value tuple within an account.\n            e.HasIndex(x => new { x.AccountId, x.Location, x.EventType, x.OccurredAt })\n             .HasDatabaseName(\"IX_activity_events_dedup\");\n        });\n\n        model.Entity<SeedRun>(e =>\n        {\n            e.ToTable(\"seed_runs\");\n            e.HasKey(x => x.Id);\n            e.Property(x => x.Id).HasColumnName(\"id\").ValueGeneratedOnAdd();\n            e.Property(x => x.SourceFile).HasColumnName(\"source_file\").HasMaxLength(260).IsRequired();\n            e.Property(x => x.Sha256).HasColumnName(\"sha256\").HasMaxLength(64).IsRequired();\n            e.Property(x => x.AccountRows).HasColumnName(\"account_rows\");\n            e.Property(x => x.EventRows).HasColumnName(\"event_rows\");\n            e.Property(x => x.AppliedAtUtc).HasColumnName(\"applied_at_utc\").HasColumnType(\"datetime2(0)\");\n        });\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "file_path": "c:\\repos\\personal\\qualitara\\home-challenge\\backend\\src\\Relay.Api\\Data\\Seeding\\TimeZoneSegmenter.cs",
  "content": "namespace Relay.Api.Data.Seeding;\n\n/// <summary>A span of UTC time during which an account's zone has one constant offset.</summary>\npublic readonly record struct UtcOffsetSegment(DateTime FromUtc, DateTime ToUtcExclusive, int OffsetMinutes);\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Splits a UTC range into the periods where a given IANA zone holds a constant offset.\n/// This is the whole of the timezone reasoning, deliberately kept as pure C# with no\n/// database involved so it is unit-testable — the seed range spans the 2026-03-08 DST\n/// transition, so a single fixed offset per account would be wrong.\n/// </summary>\npublic static class TimeZoneSegmenter\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Scans hourly. DST transitions in every zone used by the dataset occur on the hour,\n    /// so hourly resolution places each boundary exactly rather than approximately.\n    /// A daily scan would misplace a transition by up to a day and mis-bucket events.\n    /// </summary>\n    public static IReadOnlyList<UtcOffsetSegment> Segment(TimeZoneInfo zone, DateTime fromUtc, DateTime toUtcExclusive)\n    {\n        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(zone);\n        if (toUtcExclusive <= fromUtc) return [];\n\n        var start = DateTime.SpecifyKind(fromUtc, DateTimeKind.Utc);\n        var end = DateTime.SpecifyKind(toUtcExclusive, DateTimeKind.Utc);\n\n        var segments = new List<UtcOffsetSegment>();\n        var segmentStart = start;\n        var segmentOffset = OffsetMinutesAt(zone, start);\n\n        for (var cursor = start.AddHours(1); cursor < end; cursor = cursor.AddHours(1))\n        {\n            var offset = OffsetMinutesAt(zone, cursor);\n            if (offset == segmentOffset) continue;\n\n            segments.Add(new UtcOffsetSegment(segmentStart, cursor, segmentOffset));\n            segmentStart = cursor;\n            segmentOffset = offset;\n        }\n\n        segments.Add(new UtcOffsetSegment(segmentStart, end, segmentOffset));\n        return segments;\n    }\n\n    private static int OffsetMinutesAt(TimeZoneInfo zone, DateTime utcInstant)\n        => (int)zone.GetUtcOffset(utcInstant).TotalMinutes;\n}\n"
}

  "file_path": "c:\\repos\\personal\\qualitara\\home-challenge\\backend\\src\\Relay.Api\\Data\\Seeding\\SeedLoader.cs",
  "content": "using System.Security.Cryptography;\nusing System.Text;\nusing Microsoft.Data.SqlClient;\nusing Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;\nusing Relay.Api.Data.Entities;\n\nnamespace Relay.Api.Data.Seeding;\n\npublic enum SeedResult { Applied, AlreadyApplied, SkippedDataPresent }\n\npublic sealed record SeedOutcome(SeedResult Result, int AccountRows, int EventRows, string Sha256);\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Loads the provided seed.sql without modifying it.\n///\n/// The file is ~12.6k bare INSERTs carrying explicit ids, so a second run would violate\n/// the primary key. The brief says to treat it as production data, so idempotency comes\n/// from wrapping the load — a hash-stamped marker row in seed_runs — rather than from\n/// rewriting the file with IF NOT EXISTS guards.\n/// </summary>\npublic sealed class SeedLoader(RelayDbContext db, ILogger<SeedLoader> logger)\n{\n    private const int StatementsPerBatch = 500;\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Derives the account-local calendar date and Monday week start for one constant-offset\n    /// span. Set-based: one statement per (account, DST segment), so ~2 per account rather\n    /// than 12.6k row updates. 1900-01-01 was a Monday, which makes the week start\n    /// arithmetic independent of the connection's DATEFIRST setting.\n    /// </summary>\n    private const string FillLocalDatesSql = \"\"\"\n        UPDATE e\n           SET occurred_local_date = d.local_date,\n               local_week_start    = DATEADD(DAY, -(DATEDIFF(DAY, '19000101', d.local_date) % 7), d.local_date)\n          FROM activity_events AS e\n         CROSS APPLY (SELECT CAST(DATEADD(MINUTE, @offsetMinutes, e.occurred_at) AS date)) AS d(local_date)\n         WHERE e.account_id  = @accountId\n           AND e.occurred_at >= @fromUtc\n           AND e.occurred_at <  @toUtc;\n        \"\"\";\n\n    public async Task<SeedOutcome> EnsureSeededAsync(string seedSqlPath, CancellationToken ct = default)\n    {\n        if (!File.Exists(seedSqlPath))\n            throw new FileNotFoundException($\"Seed file not found. Expected it at '{seedSqlPath}'.\", seedSqlPath);\n\n        var sql = await File.ReadAllTextAsync(seedSqlPath, ct);\n        var sha = Convert.ToHexString(SHA256.HashData(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(sql))).ToLowerInvariant();\n\n        if (await db.SeedRuns.AnyAsync(r => r.Sha256 == sha, ct))\n        {\n            var existing = await db.SeedRuns.AsNoTracking().FirstAsync(r => r.Sha256 == sha, ct);\n            logger.LogInformation(\"Seed {Sha} already applied at {At}; skipping.\", sha[..12], existing.AppliedAtUtc);\n            return new SeedOutcome(SeedResult.AlreadyApplied, existing.AccountRows, existing.EventRows, sha);\n        }\n\n        // Data from some other source is present. Refuse rather than half-load on top of it.\n        if (await db.Accounts.AnyAsync(ct))\n        {\n            logger.LogWarning(\"Tables already hold data that did not come from seed {Sha}; skipping load.\", sha[..12]);\n            return new SeedOutcome(SeedResult.SkippedDataPresent, 0, 0, sha);\n        }\n\n        await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync(ct);\n\n        await ExecuteSeedStatementsAsync(sql, ct);\n        await FillLocalDatesAsync(ct);\n        await AssertNoUnbucketedEventsAsync(ct);\n\n        var accountRows = await db.Accounts.CountAsync(ct);\n        var eventRows = await db.ActivityEvents.CountAsync(ct);\n\n        db.SeedRuns.Add(new SeedRun\n        {\n            SourceFile = Path.GetFileName(seedSqlPath),\n            Sha256 = sha,\n            AccountRows = accountRows,\n            EventRows = eventRows,\n            AppliedAtUtc = DateTime.UtcNow\n        });\n        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);\n        await tx.CommitAsync(ct);\n\n        logger.LogInformation(\"Seeded {Accounts} accounts and {Events} events from {File}.\",\n            accountRows, eventRows, Path.GetFileName(seedSqlPath));\n\n        return new SeedOutcome(SeedResult.Applied, accountRows, eventRows, sha);\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>Executes the file in batches. One round trip per statement would be 12.6k\n    /// round trips; the file has no GO separators and every statement is one line.</summary>\n    private async Task ExecuteSeedStatementsAsync(string sql, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var statements = sql\n            .Split('\\n')\n            .Select(line => line.Trim())\n            .Where(line => line.Length > 0 && !line.StartsWith(\"--\", StringComparison.Ordinal))\n            .ToList();\n\n        foreach (var batch in statements.Chunk(StatementsPerBatch))\n            await db.Database.ExecuteSqlRawAsync(string.Join('\\n', batch), ct);\n    }\n\n    private async Task FillLocalDatesAsync(CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var accounts = await db.Accounts.AsNoTracking().ToListAsync(ct);\n\n        foreach (var account in accounts)\n        {\n            var bounds = await db.ActivityEvents\n                .Where(e => e.AccountId == account.Id)\n                .GroupBy(_ => 1)\n                .Select(g => new { Min = g.Min(e => e.OccurredAt), Max = g.Max(e => e.OccurredAt) })\n                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct);\n\n            // Quiet Harbor Spa (id 20) has no events at all. Not an error — an empty account\n            // is a case the dashboard has to render, so it must survive seeding untouched.\n            if (bounds is null) continue;\n\n            var zone = TimeZoneInfo.FindSystemTimeZoneById(account.Timezone);\n            var segments = TimeZoneSegmenter.Segment(zone, bounds.Min, bounds.Max.AddSeconds(1));\n\n            foreach (var segment in segments)\n            {\n                await db.Database.ExecuteSqlRawAsync(FillLocalDatesSql,\n                [\n                    new SqlParameter(\"@offsetMinutes\", segment.OffsetMinutes),\n                    new SqlParameter(\"@accountId\", account.Id),\n                    new SqlParameter(\"@fromUtc\", segment.FromUtc),\n                    new SqlParameter(\"@toUtc\", segment.ToUtcExclusive)\n                ], ct);\n            }\n\n            logger.LogDebug(\"Account {Id} ({Zone}): {Segments} offset segment(s).\",\n                account.Id, account.Timezone, segments.Count);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>The local-date columns are nullable only because seed.sql cannot supply them.\n    /// Nothing downstream tolerates a null, so a gap is a hard failure at load time rather\n    /// than a silently missing row in an aggregate.</summary>\n    private async Task AssertNoUnbucketedEventsAsync(CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var unbucketed = await db.ActivityEvents.CountAsync(e => e.OccurredLocalDate == null, ct);\n        if (unbucketed > 0)\n            throw new InvalidOperationException(\n                $\"{unbucketed} event(s) have no local date after seeding. Every event must fall inside \" +\n                \"one of its account's offset segments; a gap means the segmentation is wrong.\");\n    }\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "c:\\repos\\personal\\qualitara\\home-challenge\\backend\\src\\Relay.Api\\Analytics\\Sql\\WeeklyTotalsByLocation.sql",
  "content": "-- Weekly event totals per location, for one account, across the current week and the\n-- N baseline weeks immediately before it.\n--\n-- Contract with the C# caller (PLAN.md Amendment 2): SQL aggregates as far as weekly\n-- totals and stops there. Median, MAD, the typical band and the status are computed in\n-- C# over this result set, which is at most (locations x weeks) rows -- 15 x 13 for the\n-- largest account in the seed.\n--\n-- Parameters:\n--   @accountId        int   -- always scoped: 'Site A' exists in 19 different accounts\n--   @currentWeekStart date  -- Monday of the week under review, account-local\n--   @windowStart      date  -- Monday of the oldest baseline week (computed by the caller\n--                              so this file stays a single composable statement)\n--   @baselineWeeks    int   -- 4 | 8 | 12\n--   @eventType        nvarchar(40) -- 'all', or one of the three event types\n--\n-- Three things this has to get right, each measured in analysis/:\n--   1. DEDUPLICATION. 12 exact value-duplicates carry distinct ids. Counting both copies\n--      inflates whichever baseline week they land in.\n--   2. GAP FILLING. GROUP BY returns only weeks that have rows. A location that went\n--      silent for a week must produce 0, not disappear -- a median taken over 5 of 8\n--      weeks is wrong and looks perfectly fine.\n--   3. LOCAL WEEKS. Buckets come from local_week_start, derived at load time in the\n--      account's own timezone, never from occurred_at directly.\n\nWITH week_offsets AS (\n    -- Recursive rather than GENERATE_SERIES: that function needs compatibility level 160,\n    -- and this stays runnable on older SQL Server. Depth is at most 12.\n    SELECT 0 AS n\n    UNION ALL\n    SELECT n + 1 FROM week_offsets WHERE n < @baselineWeeks\n),\nweeks AS (\n    SELECT CAST(DATEADD(WEEK, -n, @currentWeekStart) AS date) AS week_start\n    FROM week_offsets\n),\nlocations AS (\n    -- Any location with activity somewhere in the window. One that has baseline volume but\n    -- went quiet this week still appears -- that is precisely the location needing\n    -- attention. One with no activity anywhere in the window drops off instead of reading\n    -- as \"-100%\" forever.\n    SELECT DISTINCT location\n    FROM activity_events\n    WHERE account_id = @accountId\n      AND local_week_start BETWEEN @windowStart AND @currentWeekStart\n),\nspine AS (\n    SELECT l.location, w.week_start\n    FROM locations AS l\n    CROSS JOIN weeks AS w\n),\ndeduped AS (\n    -- Keep the lowest id of each exact value tuple. GROUP BY treats NULLs as equal, which\n    -- is what we want: duplicates with a NULL outcome or duration must collapse too.\n    SELECT MIN(id) AS id\n    FROM activity_events\n    WHERE account_id = @accountId\n      AND local_week_start BETWEEN @windowStart AND @currentWeekStart\n    GROUP BY location, event_type, occurred_at, duration_seconds, outcome\n),\ntotals AS (\n    SELECT e.location,\n           e.local_week_start AS week_start,\n           COUNT_BIG(*) AS event_count\n    FROM activity_events AS e\n    INNER JOIN deduped AS d ON d.id = e.id\n    WHERE @eventType = 'all' OR e.event_type = @eventType\n    GROUP BY e.location, e.local_week_start\n)\nSELECT s.location                              AS Location,\n       s.week_start                            AS WeekStart,\n       CAST(ISNULL(t.event_count, 0) AS int)   AS EventCount\nFROM spine AS s\nLEFT JOIN totals AS t\n       ON t.location   = s.location\n      AND t.week_start = s.week_start\nORDER BY s.location, s.week_start;\n"
}
```

  "file_path": "c:\\repos\\personal\\qualitara\\home-challenge\\backend\\src\\Relay.Api\\Analytics\\BaselineCalculator.cs",
  "content": "namespace Relay.Api.Analytics;\n\npublic enum PulseStatus\n{\n    /// <summary>Inside the typical band.</summary>\n    Normal,\n    Above,\n    Below,\n\n    /// <summary>Baseline median below the low-volume threshold. Counts and band are still\n    /// shown, but no status badge: a location going 5 -> 9 is \"+64%\" and is also noise.</summary>\n    LowVolume,\n\n    /// <summary>Fewer complete weeks of history than the baseline needs to mean anything.</summary>\n    InsufficientHistory,\n\n    /// <summary>Baseline median is zero. There is no percentage change from zero.</summary>\n    NoBaseline\n}\n\npublic sealed record BaselineOptions\n{\n    public int MinHistoryWeeks { get; init; } = 4;\n    public int LowVolumeThreshold { get; init; } = 5;\n\n    /// <summary>Half-width of the typical band, in robust standard deviations. 2 gives\n    /// roughly a 95% band for normally distributed counts.</summary>\n    public double BandSigmas { get; init; } = 2.0;\n\n    public static BaselineOptions Default { get; } = new();\n}\n\npublic sealed record BaselineAssessment\n{\n    public required int Current { get; init; }\n    public required int BaselineWeeksUsed { get; init; }\n    public required double BaselineMedian { get; init; }\n    public required double Mad { get; init; }\n    public required double Spread { get; init; }\n    public required double TypicalLow { get; init; }\n    public required double TypicalHigh { get; init; }\n\n    /// <summary>Null when there is no meaningful baseline to divide by. Never infinity,\n    /// never NaN, never a fabricated -100%.</summary>\n    public double? DeltaPct { get; init; }\n\n    /// <summary>Signed distance from the baseline in spread units. Used to rank locations\n    /// by how unusual they are rather than by raw volume. Null when not comparable.</summary>\n    public double? DeviationScore { get; init; }\n\n    public required PulseStatus Status { get; init; }\n}\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Turns a location's weekly totals into \"is this normal for us?\".\n///\n/// Deliberately pure and database-free (PLAN.md Amendment 2) so every branch is unit\n/// testable. Two of the branches — InsufficientHistory and NoBaseline — have no real data\n/// behind them anywhere in the seed (minimum baseline median across all 69 account/location\n/// pairs is 3.5, minimum history 24 weeks), so their tests use synthetic fixtures. That is\n/// recorded in PLAN.md Amendment 1 rather than papered over.\n/// </summary>\npublic static class BaselineCalculator\n{\n    /// <summary>Scales MAD to a standard-deviation equivalent for a normal distribution.</summary>\n    private const double MadToSigma = 0.6745;\n\n    public static BaselineAssessment Assess(\n        int current,\n        IReadOnlyList<int> baselineWeeklyTotals,\n        BaselineOptions? options = null)\n    {\n        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(baselineWeeklyTotals);\n        var opts = options ?? BaselineOptions.Default;\n\n        if (baselineWeeklyTotals.Count < opts.MinHistoryWeeks)\n        {\n            return new BaselineAssessment\n            {\n                Current = current,\n                BaselineWeeksUsed = baselineWeeklyTotals.Count,\n                BaselineMedian = 0,\n                Mad = 0,\n                Spread = 0,\n                TypicalLow = 0,\n                TypicalHigh = 0,\n                DeltaPct = null,\n                DeviationScore = null,\n                Status = PulseStatus.InsufficientHistory\n            };\n        }\n\n        // Median, not mean. Account 6 carries 805 events on 2026-06-03 against a typical\n        // 10.9/day; that single day moves a mean-based weekly baseline by ~3.6x and leaves\n        // the median untouched. Seven of the eight low-volume locations in the seed carry\n        // that burst week inside their default baseline window, so this is not theoretical.\n        var median = Median(baselineWeeklyTotals);\n\n        if (median == 0)\n        {\n            return new BaselineAssessment\n            {\n                Current = current,\n                BaselineWeeksUsed = baselineWeeklyTotals.Count,\n                BaselineMedian = 0,\n                Mad = 0,\n                Spread = 0,\n                TypicalLow = 0,\n                TypicalHigh = 0,\n                DeltaPct = null,          // dividing by zero is not a percentage\n                DeviationScore = null,\n                Status = PulseStatus.NoBaseline\n            };\n        }\n\n        var mad = Median(baselineWeeklyTotals.Select(x => Math.Abs(x - median)).ToList());\n\n        // MAD collapses to zero whenever a MAJORITY of weeks share a value, not only when\n        // all of them do — account 12 / Site A reads [8,8,8,8,3,6,8,14], median 8, MAD 0,\n        // with real spread present. Without a fallback that location would flag 3 and 14 as\n        // extreme. sqrt(median) is the Poisson-ish spread for count data.\n        var spread = mad > 0 ? mad / MadToSigma : Math.Sqrt(median);\n\n        var halfWidth = opts.BandSigmas * spread;\n        var typicalLow = Math.Max(0, median - halfWidth);   // a week cannot hold fewer than 0 events\n        var typicalHigh = median + halfWidth;\n\n        var status = median < opts.LowVolumeThreshold\n            ? PulseStatus.LowVolume\n            : current < typicalLow ? PulseStatus.Below\n            : current > typicalHigh ? PulseStatus.Above\n            : PulseStatus.Normal;\n\n        return new BaselineAssessment\n        {\n            Current = current,\n            BaselineWeeksUsed = baselineWeeklyTotals.Count,\n            BaselineMedian = median,\n            Mad = mad,\n            Spread = spread,\n            TypicalLow = typicalLow,\n            TypicalHigh = typicalHigh,\n            DeltaPct = (current - median) / median,\n            DeviationScore = spread > 0 ? (current - median) / spread : 0,\n            Status = status\n        };\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>Even-sized sets take the mean of the two central values.</summary>\n    public static double Median(IReadOnlyList<int> values)\n    {\n        if (values.Count == 0) throw new ArgumentException(\"Median of an empty set is undefined.\", nameof(values));\n\n        var sorted = values.OrderBy(v => v).ToArray();\n        var mid = sorted.Length / 2;\n        return sorted.Length % 2 == 1\n            ? sorted[mid]\n            : (sorted[mid - 1] + sorted[mid]) / 2.0;\n    }\n\n    private static double Median(IReadOnlyList<double> values)\n    {\n        var sorted = values.OrderBy(v => v).ToArray();\n        var mid = sorted.Length / 2;\n        return sorted.Length % 2 == 1\n            ? sorted[mid]\n            : (sorted[mid - 1] + sorted[mid]) / 2.0;\n    }\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "c:\\repos\\personal\\qualitara\\home-challenge\\backend\\src\\Relay.Api\\Analytics\\WeekMath.cs",
  "content": "namespace Relay.Api.Analytics;\n\n/// <summary>Monday-start week arithmetic, in whatever calendar the caller already resolved\n/// to (always account-local here — never UTC).</summary>\npublic static class WeekMath\n{\n    public static DateOnly MondayOf(DateOnly date)\n        => date.AddDays(-(((int)date.DayOfWeek + 6) % 7));\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// The most recent Monday–Sunday week that is fully covered by data ending on\n    /// <paramref name=\"lastLocalDate\"/>. The seed ends on Monday 2026-07-27, so this\n    /// returns 2026-07-20 — the week just gone, which is what a customer admin reviews on\n    /// a Monday morning. Anchoring to the system clock instead would render an empty\n    /// dashboard, since the data is months behind wall time.\n    /// </summary>\n    public static DateOnly LastCompleteWeekStart(DateOnly lastLocalDate)\n    {\n        var monday = MondayOf(lastLocalDate);\n        return lastLocalDate >= monday.AddDays(6)\n            ? monday                 // data runs through Sunday: that week is complete\n            : monday.AddDays(-7);    // partial week in progress: fall back one\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "using System.Data;\nusing System.Reflection;\nusing Microsoft.Data.SqlClient;\nusing Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;\nusing Relay.Api.Data;\n\nnamespace Relay.Api.Analytics;\n\n/// <summary>One row of the SQL aggregate. A flat DTO, deliberately not an entity, so EF\n/// change tracking never touches it.</summary>\npublic sealed class WeeklyLocationTotal\n{\n    public string Location { get; set; } = string.Empty;\n    public DateOnly WeekStart { get; set; }\n    public int EventCount { get; set; }\n}\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Runs the weekly-totals aggregate. The SQL lives in its own embedded .sql file rather\n/// than in a string literal (PLAN.md Amendment 4) so it can be executed straight against\n/// the database to verify its numbers — which matters, because correct aggregates are the\n/// thing being judged.\n/// </summary>\npublic sealed class WeeklyTotalsQuery(RelayDbContext db)\n{\n    public static readonly string[] AllowedEventTypes =\n        [\"all\", \"call_received\", \"lead_created\", \"appointment_set\"];\n\n    private static readonly string Sql = EmbeddedSql.Load(\"Analytics.Sql.WeeklyTotalsByLocation.sql\");\n\n    /// <summary>Called at startup so a missing or renamed resource fails immediately with a\n    /// useful message rather than on the first request.</summary>\n    public static void EnsureSqlLoaded() => _ = Sql.Length;\n\n    public Task<List<WeeklyLocationTotal>> RunAsync(\n        int accountId,\n        DateOnly currentWeekStart,\n        int baselineWeeks,\n        string eventType,\n        CancellationToken ct = default)\n    {\n        if (!AllowedEventTypes.Contains(eventType))\n            throw new ArgumentException($\"Unsupported event type '{eventType}'.\", nameof(eventType));\n\n        // Computed here rather than DECLAREd in the .sql so that file stays a single\n        // statement — EF composes over raw SQL, and a multi-statement batch would break it.\n        var windowStart = currentWeekStart.AddDays(-7 * baselineWeeks);\n\n        return db.Database.SqlQueryRaw<WeeklyLocationTotal>(Sql,\n            new SqlParameter(\"@accountId\", SqlDbType.Int) { Value = accountId },\n            new SqlParameter(\"@currentWeekStart\", SqlDbType.Date) { Value = currentWeekStart.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue) },\n            new SqlParameter(\"@windowStart\", SqlDbType.Date) { Value = windowStart.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue) },\n            new SqlParameter(\"@baselineWeeks\", SqlDbType.Int) { Value = baselineWeeks },\n            new SqlParameter(\"@eventType\", SqlDbType.NVarChar, 40) { Value = eventType }\n        ).ToListAsync(ct);\n    }\n}\n\ninternal static class EmbeddedSql\n{\n    public static string Load(string relativeName)\n    {\n        var assembly = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly();\n        var resourceName = $\"{assembly.GetName().Name}.{relativeName}\";\n\n        using var stream = assembly.GetManifestResourceStream(resourceName);\n        if (stream is null)\n        {\n            var available = string.Join(Environment.NewLine + \"  \", assembly.GetManifestResourceNames());\n            throw new InvalidOperationException(\n                $\"Embedded SQL resource '{resourceName}' was not found. Resource names are derived from \" +\n                $\"the file path, so a moved or renamed .sql breaks this silently at runtime.{Environment.NewLine}\" +\n                $\"Available resources:{Environment.NewLine}  {available}\");\n        }\n\n        using var reader = new StreamReader(stream);\n        return reader.ReadToEnd();\n    }\n}\n"

DeltaPct,\n    double? DeviationScore,\n    int BaselineWeeksUsed,\n    string Status);\n\npublic sealed record LocationPulse(string Location, MetricView Metric);\n\n/// <summary>Surfaced rather than hidden: the API says out loud how many rows it dropped and\n/// whether the account has any data at all, so a reader can tell an empty account from a\n/// broken query.</summary>\npublic sealed record DataQuality(\n    bool HasData,\n    int DuplicateEventsExcluded,\n    DateOnly?   "content": "using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;\nusing Relay.Api.Contracts;\nusing Relay.Api.Data;\n\nnamespace Relay.Api.Analytics;\n\npublic sealed class PulseService(RelayDbContext db, WeeklyTotalsQuery totals)\n{\n    public static readonly int[] AllowedBaselineWeeks = [4, 8, 12];\n\n    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<AccountListItem>> ListAccountsAsync(CancellationToken ct = default)\n        => await db.Accounts\n            .AsNoTracking()\n            .OrderBy(a => a.Name)\n            .Select(a => new AccountListItem(\n                a.Id,\n                a.Name,\n                a.Industry,\n                a.Timezone,\n                a.Events.Select(e => e.Location).Distinct().Count(),\n                a.Events.Any(),\n                a.Events.Max(e => e.OccurredLocalDate)))\n            .ToListAsync(ct);\n\n    /// <summary>Returns null when the account does not exist. An account that exists but has\n    /// no events is NOT null — it is a real state the dashboard has to render, and Quiet\n    /// Harbor Spa in the seed is exactly that.</summary>\n    public async Task<WeeklyPulseResponse?> GetWeeklyPulseAsync(\n        int accountId,\n        DateOnly? requestedWeekStart,\n        int baselineWeeks,\n        string eventType,\n        CancellationToken ct = default)\n    {\n        var account = await db.Accounts.AsNoTracking()\n            .Where(a => a.Id == accountId)\n            .Select(a => new { a.Id, a.Name, a.Industry, a.Timezone })\n            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct);\n\n        if (account is null) return null;\n\n        var bounds = await db.ActivityEvents\n            .Where(e => e.AccountId == accountId)\n            .GroupBy(_ => 1)\n            .Select(g => new\n            {\n                Earliest = g.Min(e => e.OccurredLocalDate),\n                Latest = g.Max(e => e.OccurredLocalDate),\n                Locations = g.Select(e => e.Location).Distinct().Count()\n            })\n            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct);\n\n        var hasData = bounds is not null && bounds.Latest is not null;\n\n        // \"Now\" comes from the data, never from the clock — the seed ends 2026-07-27 and the\n        // system clock would produce an empty dashboard. When the account itself is empty we\n        // still need a week to render, so fall back to the newest date anywhere in the set.\n        var anchorDate = bounds?.Latest\n                         ?? await db.ActivityEvents.MaxAsync(e => e.OccurredLocalDate, ct);\n\n        var weekStart = requestedWeekStart is { } requested\n            ? WeekMath.MondayOf(requested)\n            : anchorDate is { } anchor\n                ? WeekMath.LastCompleteWeekStart(anchor)\n                : WeekMath.LastCompleteWeekStart(DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.UtcNow));\n\n        var windowStart = weekStart.AddDays(-7 * baselineWeeks);\n\n        var accountItem = new AccountListItem(\n            account.Id, account.Name, account.Industry, account.Timezone,\n            bounds?.Locations ?? 0, hasData, bounds?.Latest);\n\n        var week = new WeekWindow(weekStart, weekStart.AddDays(6));\n        var baselineWindow = new BaselineWindow(baselineWeeks, windowStart, weekStart.AddDays(-1));\n\n        if (!hasData)\n        {\n            return new WeeklyPulseResponse(\n                accountItem, week, baselineWindow, eventType,\n                Total: null,\n                Locations: [],\n                DataQuality: new DataQuality(false, 0, null, null));\n        }\n\n        var rows = await totals.RunAsync(accountId, weekStart, baselineWeeks, eventType, ct);\n        var duplicates = await CountDuplicatesInWindowAsync(accountId, windowStart, weekStart, ct);\n\n        var locations = rows\n            .GroupBy(r => r.Location, StringComparer.Ordinal)\n            .Select(g =>\n            {\n                var current = g.FirstOrDefault(r => r.WeekStart == weekStart)?.EventCount ?? 0;\n                var baseline = g.Where(r => r.WeekStart < weekStart)\n                                .OrderBy(r => r.WeekStart)\n                                .Select(r => r.EventCount)\n                                .ToList();\n                return new LocationPulse(g.Key, ToView(BaselineCalculator.Assess(current, baseline)));\n            })\n            .OrderBy(l => ActionPriority(l.Metric.Status))\n            .ThenByDescending(l => Math.Abs(l.Metric.DeviationScore ?? 0))\n            .ThenBy(l => l.Location, StringComparer.Ordinal)\n            .ToList();\n\n        // The account roll-up is the same comparison applied to the sum across locations.\n        // Summing the gap-filled per-location totals is what makes this correct: a location\n        // missing from a week contributes 0 rather than being absent from the sum.\n        var byWeek = rows.GroupBy(r => r.WeekStart)\n                         .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.Sum(r => r.EventCount));\n\n        var accountCurrent = byWeek.GetValueOrDefault(weekStart);\n        var accountBaseline = byWeek.Where(kv => kv.Key < weekStart)\n                                    .OrderBy(kv => kv.Key)\n                                    .Select(kv => kv.Value)\n                                    .ToList();\n\n        return new WeeklyPulseResponse(\n            accountItem, week, baselineWindow, eventType,\n            Total: ToView(BaselineCalculator.Assess(accountCurrent, accountBaseline)),\n            Locations: locations,\n            DataQuality: new DataQuality(true, duplicates, bounds!.Earliest, bounds.Latest));\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Diagnostic only — the pulse aggregate itself runs through the embedded SQL. Reported\n    /// so the deduplication is visible in the payload instead of silently dropping rows.\n    /// </summary>\n    private async Task<int> CountDuplicatesInWindowAsync(\n        int accountId, DateOnly windowStart, DateOnly weekStart, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var inWindow = db.ActivityEvents.Where(e =>\n            e.AccountId == accountId &&\n            e.LocalWeekStart >= windowStart &&\n            e.LocalWeekStart <= weekStart);\n\n        var raw = await inWindow.CountAsync(ct);\n        var distinct = await inWindow\n            .Select(e => new { e.Location, e.EventType, e.OccurredAt, e.DurationSeconds, e.Outcome })\n            .Distinct()\n            .CountAsync(ct);\n\n        return raw - distinct;\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>Locations that need a decision come first; suppressed ones sink.</summary>\n    private static int ActionPriority(string status) => status switch\n    {\n        \"below\" or \"above\" => 0,\n        \"normal\" => 1,\n        \"lowVolume\" => 2,\n        _ => 3\n    };\n\n    private static MetricView ToView(BaselineAssessment a) => new(\n        a.Current,\n        Math.Round(a.BaselineMedian, 2),\n        Math.Round(a.TypicalLow, 2),\n        Math.Round(a.TypicalHigh, 2),\n        a.DeltaPct is { } d ? Math.Round(d, 4) : null,\n        a.DeviationScore is { } s ? Math.Round(s, 3) : null,\n        a.BaselineWeeksUsed,\n        Camel(a.Status.ToString()));\n\n    private static string Camel(string s) => char.ToLowerInvariant(s[0]) + s[1..];\n}\n"
}

## ASSISTANT  <sub>08/14/2026 01:07:20</sub>

El roll-up de cuenta verificado a mano contra la serie cruda:

```
baseline (8 sem) : 53, 881, 102, 59, 76, 69, 79, 50
ordenado         : 50, 53, 59, 69, 76, 79, 102, 881   → mediana = (69+76)/2 = 72.5  ✓
desviaciones     : 3.5, 3.5, 6.5, 13.5, 19.5, 22.5, 29.5, 808.5 → MAD = 16.5  ✓
spread           : 16.5/0.6745 = 24.46 → banda = 72.5 ± 48.93 = [23.57, 121.43]  ✓
actual 87        → dentro de banda → normal, +20%  ✓
```

**Y ahí está la demostración con datos reales:** la semana del burst (881) está dentro del baseline. Una media daría `1369/8 = 171,1`, y esta semana leería **−49%, colapso**. La mediana dice **+20%, normal**. Esa es la pantalla por defecto de la cuenta 6, no un fixture.

Casos límite: cuenta 20 devuelve `hasData=false`, `total=null`, 0 sedes y una semana válida — no un 500 ni un `NaN`. Cuenta 16 (una sola sede, la más dispersa) resuelve limpio.

{
  "file_path": "c:\\repos\\personal\\qualitara\\home-challenge\\backend\\tests\\Relay.Api.Tests\\BaselineCalculatorTests.cs",
  "content": "using Relay.Api.Analytics;\n\nnamespace Relay.Api.Tests;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// The statistics that decide what a customer is told. No database — that split is the\n/// point of PLAN.md Amendment 2.\n///\n/// Where a fixture is a real series from the seed it says so and cites the account and\n/// location; those numbers come from analysis/, never from intuition. Two branches\n/// (InsufficientHistory, NoBaseline) have no real data behind them anywhere in the dataset,\n/// so they use synthetic fixtures — recorded in PLAN.md Amendment 1 rather than dressed up\n/// as coverage of real rows.\n/// </summary>\npublic class BaselineCalculatorTests\n{\n    // Account 6 / Site J, default window. The 56 is the planted burst week.\n    private static readonly int[] SiteJWithBurst = [4, 56, 11, 2, 3, 3, 5, 3];\n\n    // Account 12 / Site A, default window. A majority of weeks share the value 8.\n    private static readonly int[] SiteAMajorityIdentical = [8, 8, 8, 8, 3, 6, 8, 14];\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void Burst_week_does_not_move_the_baseline()\n    {\n        var withBurst = BaselineCalculator.Assess(5, SiteJWithBurst);\n        var withoutBurst = BaselineCalculator.Assess(5, [4, 11, 2, 3, 3, 5, 3]);\n\n        Assert.Equal(3.5, withBurst.BaselineMedian);\n\n        // The mean of the same series is 10.875 -- more than 3x the median. A mean-based\n        // baseline would report this normal week as a 54% collapse.\n        Assert.Equal(10.875, SiteJWithBurst.Average(), 3);\n        Assert.True(withBurst.DeltaPct > 0, \"Median baseline sees this week as above normal.\");\n\n        // Removing the outlier barely moves the median: that is the property being bought.\n        Assert.InRange(Math.Abs(withBurst.BaselineMedian - withoutBurst.BaselineMedian), 0, 0.5);\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void Silent_weeks_count_as_zero_rather_than_being_ignored()\n    {\n        // The SQL gap-fills; the calculator must then treat those zeros as real observations.\n        // If zeros were dropped, the median here would be 5 instead of 2.5 and a location\n        // that trades every other week would look perfectly healthy.\n        var assessment = BaselineCalculator.Assess(0, [5, 0, 5, 0, 5, 0, 5, 0]);\n\n        Assert.Equal(2.5, assessment.BaselineMedian);\n        Assert.Equal(8, assessment.BaselineWeeksUsed);\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void Zero_baseline_yields_no_percentage_at_all()\n    {\n        // Synthetic: no location in the seed has a zero baseline (minimum median is 3.5).\n        var assessment = BaselineCalculator.Assess(4, [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]);\n\n        Assert.Equal(PulseStatus.NoBaseline, assessment.Status);\n        Assert.Null(assessment.DeltaPct);          // not infinity, not NaN, not a fake +400%\n        Assert.Null(assessment.DeviationScore);\n        Assert.Equal(4, assessment.Current);       // the raw count is still reported\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void Too_little_history_is_reported_rather_than_guessed()\n    {\n        // Synthetic: every location in the seed has at least 24 complete weeks.\n        var assessment = BaselineCalculator.Assess(9, [7, 8, 6]);\n\n        Assert.Equal(PulseStatus.InsufficientHistory, assessment.Status);\n        Assert.Equal(3, assessment.BaselineWeeksUsed);\n        Assert.Null(assessment.DeltaPct);\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void Mad_of_zero_falls_back_to_a_count_based_spread()\n    {\n        // Real: account 12 / Site A. MAD collapses because a MAJORITY of weeks read 8, not\n        // because the series is flat -- there is genuine spread (3 and 14) present. Without\n        // the fallback the band would have zero width and both would flag as extreme.\n        var assessment = BaselineCalculator.Assess(7, SiteAMajorityIdentical);\n\n        Assert.Equal(8, assessment.BaselineMedian);\n        Assert.Equal(0, assessment.Mad);\n        Assert.Equal(Math.Sqrt(8), assessment.Spread, 6);\n        Assert.True(assessment.TypicalHigh > assessment.TypicalLow, \"Band must have width.\");\n        Assert.False(double.IsNaN(assessment.DeviationScore ?? 0));\n        Assert.False(double.IsInfinity(assessment.DeviationScore ?? 0));\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void Low_volume_locations_report_counts_but_suppress_the_verdict()\n    {\n        // Real: account 6 / Site J. 5 against a baseline of 3.5 is \"+43%\" and is also noise.\n        var assessment = BaselineCalculator.Assess(5, SiteJWithBurst);\n\n        Assert.Equal(PulseStatus.LowVolume, assessment.Status);\n        Assert.Equal(5, assessment.Current);\n        Assert.NotNull(assessment.DeltaPct);   // the number is still available to show\n    }\n\n    [Theory]\n    [InlineData(10, PulseStatus.Normal)]   // dead on the median\n    [InlineData(16, PulseStatus.Normal)]   // exactly on the upper edge -- inclusive\n    [InlineData(17, PulseStatus.Above)]    // one past it\n    [InlineData(4, PulseStatus.Normal)]    // exactly on the lower edge -- inclusive\n    [InlineData(3, PulseStatus.Below)]\n    public void Band_edges_are_inclusive(int current, PulseStatus expected)\n    {\n        // median 10, MAD 2 -> spread 2/0.6745 = 2.9652, band 10 +/- 5.9304 = [4.07, 15.93].\n        int[] baseline = [10, 10, 8, 12, 8, 12, 10, 10];\n        var assessment = BaselineCalculator.Assess(current, baseline);\n\n        Assert.Equal(10, assessment.BaselineMedian);\n        Assert.Equal(2, assessment.Mad);\n        Assert.Equal(expected, assessment.Status);\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void Typical_band_never_goes_below_zero()\n    {\n        // A week cannot contain a negative number of events, so the band floor is 0 even\n        // when median - 2*spread is negative.\n        var assessment = BaselineCalculator.Assess(6, [6, 1, 9, 2, 8, 3, 7, 5]);\n\n        Assert.Equal(0, assessment.TypicalLow);\n    }\n\n    [Theory]\n    [InlineData(new[] { 1, 2, 3 }, 2.0)]\n    [InlineData(new[] { 1, 2, 3, 4 }, 2.5)]\n    [InlineData(new[] { 5 }, 5.0)]\n    [InlineData(new[] { 3, 1, 2 }, 2.0)]   // unsorted input\n    public void Median_handles_odd_even_and_unsorted(int[] values, double expected)\n        => Assert.Equal(expected, BaselineCalculator.Median(values));\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void Median_of_nothing_is_an_error_not_a_zero()\n        => Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => BaselineCalculator.Median(Array.Empty<int>()));\n}\n"
}

  "file_path": "c:\\repos\\personal\\qualitara\\home-challenge\\backend\\tests\\Relay.Api.Tests\\TimeAndWeekTests.cs",
  "content": "using Relay.Api.Analytics;\nusing Relay.Api.Data.Seeding;\n\nnamespace Relay.Api.Tests;\n\npublic class WeekMathTests\n{\n    [Theory]\n    [InlineData(\"2026-07-20\", \"2026-07-20\")]   // a Monday is its own week start\n    [InlineData(\"2026-07-26\", \"2026-07-20\")]   // Sunday belongs to the week that began Monday\n    [InlineData(\"2026-07-27\", \"2026-07-27\")]   // next Monday starts a new week\n    public void MondayOf_anchors_to_the_start_of_the_iso_week(string input, string expected)\n        => Assert.Equal(DateOnly.Parse(expected), WeekMath.MondayOf(DateOnly.Parse(input)));\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void Data_ending_on_a_monday_shows_the_week_that_just_closed()\n    {\n        // The seed's last local date is Monday 2026-07-27. The week containing it is still\n        // in progress, so the dashboard must show 2026-07-20..26 -- which is exactly the\n        // Monday-morning review the ticket describes.\n        Assert.Equal(DateOnly.Parse(\"2026-07-20\"),\n            WeekMath.LastCompleteWeekStart(DateOnly.Parse(\"2026-07-27\")));\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void Data_ending_on_a_sunday_shows_that_same_week()\n    {\n        // A week whose Sunday is covered is complete and must not be skipped.\n        Assert.Equal(DateOnly.Parse(\"2026-07-20\"),\n            WeekMath.LastCompleteWeekStart(DateOnly.Parse(\"2026-07-26\")));\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void A_partial_week_is_never_shown_as_if_it_were_whole()\n    {\n        // Saturday: six of seven days present. Showing it would understate every location.\n        Assert.Equal(DateOnly.Parse(\"2026-07-13\"),\n            WeekMath.LastCompleteWeekStart(DateOnly.Parse(\"2026-07-25\")));\n    }\n}\n\npublic class TimeZoneSegmenterTests\n{\n    private static readonly DateTime SeedStart = new(2026, 2, 1, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc);\n    private static readonly DateTime SeedEnd = new(2026, 7, 28, 0, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc);\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void A_zone_with_dst_splits_the_seed_range_in_two()\n    {\n        // America/Chicago moves from -06:00 to -05:00 on 2026-03-08. One fixed offset for\n        // the whole range would mis-bucket every event on one side of that date.\n        var segments = TimeZoneSegmenter.Segment(Tz(\"America/Chicago\"), SeedStart, SeedEnd);\n\n        Assert.Equal(2, segments.Count);\n        Assert.Equal(-360, segments[0].OffsetMinutes);   // CST\n        Assert.Equal(-300, segments[1].OffsetMinutes);   // CDT\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void The_transition_lands_on_the_exact_hour_not_the_nearest_day()\n    {\n        // 2026-03-08 02:00 local == 08:00 UTC. An hourly scan places this precisely; a daily\n        // scan would be off by up to 24 hours and put real events in the wrong local day.\n        var segments = TimeZoneSegmenter.Segment(Tz(\"America/Chicago\"), SeedStart, SeedEnd);\n\n        Assert.Equal(new DateTime(2026, 3, 8, 8, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc), segments[1].FromUtc);\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void A_zone_without_dst_stays_a_single_segment()\n    {\n        // Phoenix does not observe DST, and two accounts in the seed live there.\n        var segments = TimeZoneSegmenter.Segment(Tz(\"America/Phoenix\"), SeedStart, SeedEnd);\n\n        Assert.Single(segments);\n        Assert.Equal(-420, segments[0].OffsetMinutes);\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void Utc_is_a_single_zero_offset_segment()\n    {\n        var segments = TimeZoneSegmenter.Segment(Tz(\"UTC\"), SeedStart, SeedEnd);\n\n        Assert.Single(segments);\n        Assert.Equal(0, segments[0].OffsetMinutes);\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void Segments_tile_the_whole_range_with_no_gap_and_no_overlap()\n    {\n        // A gap here means events with no local date, which the seed loader treats as a\n        // hard failure. Worth asserting directly rather than only through that guard.\n        var segments = TimeZoneSegmenter.Segment(Tz(\"America/Los_Angeles\"), SeedStart, SeedEnd);\n\n        Assert.Equal(SeedStart, segments[0].FromUtc);\n        Assert.Equal(SeedEnd, segments[^1].ToUtcExclusive);\n        for (var i = 1; i < segments.Count; i++)\n            Assert.Equal(segments[i - 1].ToUtcExclusive, segments[i].FromUtc);\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void An_empty_range_produces_no_segments()\n        => Assert.Empty(TimeZoneSegmenter.Segment(Tz(\"UTC\"), SeedStart, SeedStart));\n\n    /// <summary>IANA ids resolve on .NET 6+ regardless of host OS. This is the reason the\n    /// conversion lives in C# rather than in T-SQL, where these ids are rejected outright\n    /// (verified in PLAN.md Amendment 3).</summary>\n    private static TimeZoneInfo Tz(string ianaId) => TimeZoneInfo.FindSystemTimeZoneById(ianaId);\n}\n"
}

  "content": "using Microsoft.Data.SqlClient;\nusing Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;\nusing Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions;\nusing Relay.Api.Analytics;\nusing Relay.Api.Data;\nusing Relay.Api.Data.Seeding;\n\nnamespace Relay.Api.Tests;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Brings up a migrated, seeded database for the integration tests. Runs the real\n/// SeedLoader rather than a test-only fixture, so these tests exercise the same load path\n/// the application uses — and, because the loader is idempotent, running them against an\n/// already-seeded database is a no-op rather than a failure.\n///\n/// Requires SQL Server: `docker compose up -d --wait` from the repository root.\n/// </summary>\npublic sealed class SeededDatabaseFixture : IAsyncLifetime\n{\n    private const string DefaultConnection =\n        \"Server=localhost,11433;Database=Relay;User Id=sa;Password=Relay!Local2026;TrustServerCertificate=True;Encrypt=False\";\n\n    public RelayDbContext Db { get; private set; } = null!;\n    public PulseService Pulse { get; private set; } = null!;\n    public SeedOutcome SeedOutcome { get; private set; } = null!;\n\n    public async Task InitializeAsync()\n    {\n        var connectionString = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(\"RELAY_TEST_CONNECTION\") ?? DefaultConnection;\n\n        Db = new RelayDbContext(new DbContextOptionsBuilder<RelayDbContext>()\n            .UseSqlServer(connectionString)\n            .Options);\n\n        try\n        {\n            await Db.Database.MigrateAsync();\n        }\n        catch (SqlException ex)\n        {\n            throw new InvalidOperationException(\n                \"The integration tests need SQL Server. From the repository root run:\" +\n                Environment.NewLine + \"  docker compose up -d --wait\" +\n                Environment.NewLine + \"Override the connection with RELAY_TEST_CONNECTION if you host it elsewhere.\",\n                ex);\n        }\n\n        SeedOutcome = await new SeedLoader(Db, NullLogger<SeedLoader>.Instance).EnsureSeededAsync(FindSeedFile());\n        Pulse = new PulseService(Db, new WeeklyTotalsQuery(Db));\n    }\n\n    public async Task DisposeAsync() => await Db.DisposeAsync();\n\n    private static string FindSeedFile()\n    {\n        var dir = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);\n        while (dir is not null)\n        {\n            var candidate = Path.Combine(dir.FullName, \"seed.sql\");\n            if (File.Exists(candidate)) return candidate;\n            dir = dir.Parent;\n        }\n\n        throw new FileNotFoundException($\"seed.sql not found above '{AppContext.BaseDirectory}'.\");\n    }\n}\n\n[CollectionDefinition(Name)]\npublic sealed class SeededDatabaseCollection : ICollectionFixture<SeededDatabaseFixture>\n{\n    public const string Name = \"seeded-database\";\n}\n"
}

  "file_path": "c:\\repos\\personal\\qualitara\\home-challenge\\backend\\tests\\Relay.Api.Tests\\WeeklyPulseIntegrationTests.cs",
  "content": "using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;\nusing Relay.Api.Data.Seeding;\n\nnamespace Relay.Api.Tests;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Aggregates checked against the seed. Every expected number here was computed\n/// independently in Python first (see analysis/) — if the C# and the SQL agree with each\n/// other but disagree with these, the pipeline is wrong, which is the failure a test\n/// written from the implementation would never catch.\n/// </summary>\n[Collection(SeededDatabaseCollection.Name)]\npublic class WeeklyPulseIntegrationTests(SeededDatabaseFixture fx)\n{\n    private static readonly DateOnly DefaultWeek = new(2026, 7, 20);\n\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task Seed_loads_the_expected_shape()\n    {\n        Assert.Equal(20, await fx.Db.Accounts.CountAsync());\n        Assert.Equal(12_626, await fx.Db.ActivityEvents.CountAsync());\n        Assert.Equal(0, await fx.Db.ActivityEvents.CountAsync(e => e.OccurredLocalDate == null));\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public void Reseeding_an_already_seeded_database_is_a_no_op()\n    {\n        // seed.sql is 12.6k INSERTs with explicit ids; a second run would violate the\n        // primary key. The fixture just ran the loader against a live database.\n        Assert.Contains(fx.SeedOutcome.Result, new[] { SeedResult.Applied, SeedResult.AlreadyApplied });\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task The_planted_burst_day_is_present_and_untouched()\n    {\n        // 805 events on one day against a typical 10.9. The data is not cleaned: the median\n        // baseline is what makes it harmless.\n        var count = await fx.Db.ActivityEvents.CountAsync(e =>\n            e.AccountId == 6 &&\n            e.OccurredAt >= new DateTime(2026, 6, 3) &&\n            e.OccurredAt < new DateTime(2026, 6, 4));\n\n        Assert.Equal(805, count);\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task Exactly_twelve_exact_duplicates_exist_across_the_dataset()\n    {\n        var raw = await fx.Db.ActivityEvents.CountAsync();\n        var distinct = await fx.Db.ActivityEvents\n            .Select(e => new { e.AccountId, e.Location, e.EventType, e.OccurredAt, e.DurationSeconds, e.Outcome })\n            .Distinct()\n            .CountAsync();\n\n        Assert.Equal(12, raw - distinct);\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task Default_week_comes_from_the_data_and_not_from_the_clock()\n    {\n        // The seed ends Monday 2026-07-27, months behind wall time. Using the system clock\n        // would render an empty dashboard.\n        var pulse = await fx.Pulse.GetWeeklyPulseAsync(6, null, 8, \"all\");\n\n        Assert.NotNull(pulse);\n        Assert.Equal(DefaultWeek, pulse.Week.Start);\n        Assert.Equal(new DateOnly(2026, 7, 26), pulse.Week.End);\n        Assert.Equal(new DateOnly(2026, 5, 25), pulse.Baseline.Start);\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task Account_rollup_matches_the_hand_checked_arithmetic()\n    {\n        // Baseline weekly totals for account 6 are [53, 881, 102, 59, 76, 69, 79, 50].\n        //   median = (69 + 76) / 2                 = 72.5\n        //   MAD    = median of deviations          = 16.5\n        //   spread = 16.5 / 0.6745                 = 24.46\n        //   band   = 72.5 +/- 2 * 24.46            = [23.57, 121.43]\n        // Current week is 87 -> inside the band.\n        var pulse = await fx.Pulse.GetWeeklyPulseAsync(6, DefaultWeek, 8, \"all\");\n\n        Assert.NotNull(pulse!.Total);\n        Assert.Equal(87, pulse.Total.Current);\n        Assert.Equal(72.5, pulse.Total.BaselineMedian);\n        Assert.Equal(23.57, pulse.Total.TypicalLow, 2);\n        Assert.Equal(121.43, pulse.Total.TypicalHigh, 2);\n        Assert.Equal(\"normal\", pulse.Total.Status);\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task A_mean_baseline_would_call_this_normal_week_a_collapse()\n    {\n        // The burst week (881) sits inside the default baseline window. Mean of the eight\n        // baseline weeks is 171.125, so a mean-based comparison would report the current 87\n        // as roughly -49%. This is the single most important behavioural difference in the\n        // feature, and it is visible on the default screen rather than only in a fixture.\n        var pulse = await fx.Pulse.GetWeeklyPulseAsync(6, DefaultWeek, 8, \"all\");\n\n        int[] baselineWeeks = [53, 881, 102, 59, 76, 69, 79, 50];\n        var meanDelta = (87 - baselineWeeks.Average()) / baselineWeeks.Average();\n\n        Assert.InRange(meanDelta, -0.50, -0.48);          // what a mean would have said\n        Assert.InRange(pulse!.Total!.DeltaPct!.Value, 0.19, 0.21);   // what the median says\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task Every_location_gets_a_full_run_of_weeks_including_the_silent_ones()\n    {\n        // GROUP BY only returns weeks that have rows. If gap filling were missing, a\n        // location with a quiet week would have a shorter baseline and a wrong median.\n        var pulse = await fx.Pulse.GetWeeklyPulseAsync(6, DefaultWeek, 8, \"all\");\n\n        Assert.NotEmpty(pulse!.Locations);\n        Assert.All(pulse.Locations, l => Assert.Equal(8, l.Metric.BaselineWeeksUsed));\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task Low_volume_locations_match_the_independently_computed_medians()\n    {\n        // From analysis/03: the eight low-volume locations under the default window, seven\n        // of them account 6 sites whose baseline carries the burst week.\n        var expected = new Dictionary<string, (int Current, double Median)>\n        {\n            [\"Site J\"] = (5, 3.5),\n            [\"Site M\"] = (7, 3.5),\n            [\"Site I\"] = (6, 4.0),\n            [\"Site D\"] = (4, 4.5),\n            [\"Site G\"] = (6, 4.5),\n            [\"Site N\"] = (6, 4.5),\n            [\"Site O\"] = (8, 4.5),\n        };\n\n        var pulse = await fx.Pulse.GetWeeklyPulseAsync(6, DefaultWeek, 8, \"all\");\n        var byLocation = pulse!.Locations.ToDictionary(l => l.Location, l => l.Metric);\n\n        foreach (var (location, (current, median)) in expected)\n        {\n            Assert.Equal(current, byLocation[location].Current);\n            Assert.Equal(median, byLocation[location].BaselineMedian);\n            Assert.Equal(\"lowVolume\", byLocation[location].Status);\n        }\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task Deduplication_is_applied_and_reported()\n    {\n        // Four of the twelve duplicates belong to account 6, but only the 2026-06-03 one\n        // falls inside the default window.\n        var pulse = await fx.Pulse.GetWeeklyPulseAsync(6, DefaultWeek, 8, \"all\");\n\n        Assert.Equal(1, pulse!.DataQuality.DuplicateEventsExcluded);\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task An_account_with_no_events_renders_instead_of_failing()\n    {\n        // Quiet Harbor Spa. Not a 404, not a 500, no NaN — an empty state with a real week.\n        var pulse = await fx.Pulse.GetWeeklyPulseAsync(20, null, 8, \"all\");\n\n        Assert.NotNull(pulse);\n        Assert.False(pulse.DataQuality.HasData);\n        Assert.Null(pulse.Total);\n        Assert.Empty(pulse.Locations);\n        Assert.Equal(DefaultWeek, pulse.Week.Start);   // still shows which week it is empty for\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task An_unknown_account_is_absent_rather_than_empty()\n        => Assert.Null(await fx.Pulse.GetWeeklyPulseAsync(9999, null, 8, \"all\"));\n\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task Location_names_are_scoped_to_their_account()\n    {\n        // 'Site A' exists in 19 of the 20 accounts. An unscoped query would blend them.\n        var six = await fx.Pulse.GetWeeklyPulseAsync(6, DefaultWeek, 8, \"all\");\n        var sixteen = await fx.Pulse.GetWeeklyPulseAsync(16, DefaultWeek, 8, \"all\");\n\n        var a6 = six!.Locations.Single(l => l.Location == \"Site A\").Metric.Current;\n        var a16 = sixteen!.Locations.Single(l => l.Location == \"Site A\").Metric.Current;\n\n        Assert.Equal(9, a6);\n        Assert.Equal(8, a16);\n        Assert.Single(sixteen.Locations);   // account 16 is single-site\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task Filtering_by_event_type_reduces_every_total()\n    {\n        var all = await fx.Pulse.GetWeeklyPulseAsync(6, DefaultWeek, 8, \"all\");\n        var calls = await fx.Pulse.GetWeeklyPulseAsync(6, DefaultWeek, 8, \"call_received\");\n\n        Assert.True(calls!.Total!.Current < all!.Total!.Current);\n        Assert.Equal(all.Locations.Count, calls.Locations.Count);   // the spine is unchanged\n    }\n\n    [Fact]\n    public async Task A_wider_baseline_window_uses_more_weeks()\n    {\n        var four = await fx.Pulse.GetWeeklyPulseAsync(6, DefaultWeek, 4, \"all\");\n        var twelve = await fx.Pulse.GetWeeklyPulseAsync(6, DefaultWeek, 12, \"all\");\n\n        Assert.Equal(4, four!.Total!.BaselineWeeksUsed);\n        Assert.Equal(12, twelve!.Total!.BaselineWeeksUsed);\n        Assert.Equal(new DateOnly(2026, 6, 22), four.Baseline.Start);\n    }\n}\n"
}

**Presupuesto: 4h36.** Quedan dos requisitos obligatorios: la vista Angular y el README. Estimo ~1h15 más, lo que aterriza cerca de 6h — el techo de la banda, no por encima.

Mi recomendación para el frontend, aplicando el orden de descarte del propio plan: **una sola vista, sin librerías de estado, sin estilos más allá de lo mínimo legible**. Selector de cuenta + selector de ventana de baseline + filtro de tipo de evento, los tres en query params de la URL. Tabla de sedes ordenada por desviación. Eso cubre el requisito duro (estado que sobrevive al reload) y responde las dos preguntas del ticket.

Si el tiempo aprieta, lo primero que cae es el filtro de tipo de evento, luego el selector de ventana — exactamente como está escrito en `PLAN.md` §7.

¿Sigo con Angular, o prefieres pausar aquí y retomar después? El repo está en un estado coherente y publicable ahora mismo: si pararas hoy, el README diría honestamente que falta el frontend, y eso es mejor que un frontend a medias.

A hand-edited or stale\n * link falls back to a default rather than putting the view into an impossible state.\n */\nexport function readFilters(params: ParamMap): PulseFilters {\n  const accountId = Number(params.get('accountId'));\n  const baselineWeeks = Number(params.get('baselineWeeks'));\n  const eventType = params.get('eventType') ?? '';\n  const weekStart = params.get('weekStart');\n\n  return {\n    accountId: Number.isInteger(accountId) && accountId > 0 ? accountId : DEFAULT_FILTERS.accountId,\n    baselineWeeks: (BASELINE_WEEK_OPTIONS as readonly number[]).includes(baselineWeeks)\n      ? baselineWeeks\n      : DEFAULT_FILTERS.baselineWeeks,\n    eventType: EVENT_TYPE_OPTIONS.some((o) => o.value === eventType)\n      ? eventType\n      : DEFAULT_FILTERS.eventType,\n    weekStart: weekStart && /^\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}$/.test(weekStart) ? weekStart : null,\n  };\n}\n\n@Component({\n  selector: 'app-pulse-page',\n  standalone: true,\n  imports: [DecimalPipe],\n  templateUrl: './pulse-page.component.html',\n  styleUrl: './pulse-page.component.css',\n  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,\n})\nexport class PulsePageComponent {\n  private readonly route = inject(ActivatedRoute);\n  private readonly router = inject(Router);\n  private readonly api = inject(PulseApi);\n\n  readonly baselineOptions = BASELINE_WEEK_OPTIONS;\n  readonly eventTypeOptions = EVENT_TYPE_OPTIONS;\n\n  /**\n   * The URL is the only place this state lives. Nothing is mirrored into a component\n   * field or into localStorage, which is what makes a reload and a shared link produce\n   * the same view rather than merely a similar one.\n   */\n  readonly filters = toSignal(this.route.queryParamMap.pipe(map(readFilters)), {\n    initialValue: DEFAULT_FILTERS,\n  });\n\n  readonly accounts = toSignal(\n    this.api.accounts().pipe(catchError(() => of([]))),\n    { initialValue: [] },\n  );\n\n  private readonly view = toSignal<ViewState>(\n    toObservable(this.filters).pipe(\n      switchMap((filters) =>\n        this.api.weeklyPulse(filters).pipe(\n          map((pulse): ViewState => ({ state: 'ready', pulse })),\n          startWith<ViewState>({ state: 'loading' }),\n          catchError((err) =>\n            of<ViewState>({\n              state: 'error',\n              message:\n                err?.status === 0\n                  ? 'Cannot reach the API. Is it running on http://localhost:5080?'\n                  : err?.error?.title ?? `Request failed (${err?.status ?? 'unknown'}).`,\n            }),\n          ),\n        ),\n      ),\n    ),\n    { initialValue: { state: 'loading' } },\n  );\n\n  readonly loading = computed(() => this.view().state === 'loading');\n  readonly error = computed(() => (this.view().state === 'error' ? (this.view() as any).message : null));\n  readonly pulse = computed(() => (this.view().state === 'ready' ? (this.view() as any).pulse as WeeklyPulse : null));\n\n  /** True when the account exists but has never recorded anything — Quiet Harbor Spa. */\n  readonly isEmptyAccount = computed(() => {\n    const p = this.pulse();\n    return !!p && !p.dataQuality.hasData;\n  });\n\n  patch(changes: Partial<PulseFilters>): void {\n    this.router.navigate([], {\n      relativeTo: this.route,\n      queryParams: changes,\n      queryParamsHandling: 'merge',\n    });\n  }\n\n  /** Switching account clears the week: accounts do not necessarily share a latest week. */\n  onAccountChange(value: string): void {\n    this.patch({ accountId: Number(value), weekStart: null });\n  }\n\n  shiftWeek(deltaWeeks: number): void {\n    const current = this.pulse()?.week.start;\n    if (!current) return;\n\n    const date = new Date(`${current}T00:00:00Z`);\n    date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() + deltaWeeks * 7);\n    this.patch({ weekStart: date.toISOString().slice(0, 10) });\n  }\n\n  resetWeek(): void {\n    this.patch({ weekStart: null });\n  }\n\n  // --- presentation helpers ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  statusLabel(status: PulseStatus): string {\n    switch (status) {\n      case 'above': return 'Above normal';\n      case 'below': return 'Below normal';\n      case 'normal': return 'Normal';\n      case 'lowVolume': return 'Too few to compare';\n      case 'insufficientHistory': return 'Not enough history';\n      case 'noBaseline': return 'No baseline yet';\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Only the three comparable verdicts get a badge. Low volume, thin history and a zero\n   * baseline are shown as plain muted text: the numbers are still there to read, but the\n   * dashboard does not pretend to a judgement it cannot support. A location going 5 to 9\n   * is \"+64%\" and is also noise.\n   */\n  hasBadge(status: PulseStatus): boolean {\n    return status === 'above' || status === 'below' || status === 'normal';\n  }\n\n  deltaText(metric: MetricView): string {\n    if (metric.deltaPct === null) return '—';\n    const pct = metric.deltaPct * 100;\n    const rounded = Math.abs(pct) < 0.5 ? 0 : Math.round(pct);\n    return `${rounded > 0 ? '+' : ''}${rounded}%`;\n  }\n\n  deltaClass(metric: MetricView): string {\n    if (metric.deltaPct === null) return 'delta-none';\n    if (metric.deltaPct > 0.005) return 'delta-up';\n    if (metric.deltaPct < -0.005) return 'delta-down';\n    return 'delta-flat';\n  }\n\n  formatDate(iso: string): string {\n    return new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00Z`).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {\n      day: 'numeric', month: 'short', timeZone: 'UTC',\n    });\n  }\n}\n"
}

'' : ' (no activity)' }}</option>\n      }\n    </select>\n  </label>\n\n  <label>\n    <span>Compare against</span>\n    <select [value]=\"filters().baselineWeeks\"\n            (change)=\"patch({ baselineWeeks: +$any($event.target).value })\">\n      @for (w of baselineOptions; track w) {\n        <option [value]=\"w\">previous {{ w }} weeks</option>\n      }\n    </select>\n  </label>\n\n  <label>\n    <span>Activity</span>\n    <select [value]=\"filters().eventType\"\n            (change)=\"patch({ eventType: $any($event.target).value })\">\n      @for (t of eventTypeOptions; track t.value) {\n        <option [value]=\"t.value\">{{ t.label }}</option>\n      }\n    </select>\n  </label>\n\n  @if (pulse(); as p) {\n    <div class=\"week-nav\" role=\"group\" aria-label=\"Week\">\n      <button type=\"button\" (click)=\"shiftWeek(-1)\" title=\"Previous week\">‹</button>\n      <span class=\"week-label\">{{ formatDate(p.week.start) }} – {{ formatDate(p.week.end) }}</span>\n      <button type=\"button\" (click)=\"shiftWeek(1)\" title=\"Next week\">›</button>\n      @if (filters().weekStart) {\n        <button type=\"button\" class=\"link\" (click)=\"resetWeek()\">latest</button>\n      }\n    </div>\n  }\n</section>\n\n@if (loading()) {\n  <p class=\"notice\">Loading…</p>\n}\n\n@if (error(); as message) {\n  <p class=\"notice error\">{{ message }}</p>\n}\n\n@if (pulse(); as p) {\n  @if (isEmptyAccount()) {\n    <!-- A real account with zero recorded activity. Shown as an explicit state rather than\n         an empty table, a zero, or a percentage against nothing. -->\n    <section class=\"card empty\">\n      <h2>{{ p.account.name }}</h2>\n      <p class=\"empty-headline\">No activity recorded</p>\n      <p class=\"muted\">\n        Nothing has been logged for this account, so there is nothing to compare the week of\n        {{ formatDate(p.week.start) }} – {{ formatDate(p.week.end) }} against.\n      </p>\n    </section>\n  } @else if (p.total; as total) {\n\n    <section class=\"card rollup\">\n      <div class=\"rollup-head\">\n        <h2>{{ p.account.name }}</h2>\n        <span class=\"tz\">{{ p.account.timezone }} · {{ p.account.locationCount }} site{{ p.account.locationCount === 1 ? '' : 's' }}</span>\n      </div>\n\n      <div class=\"rollup-body\">\n        <div class=\"headline\">\n          <span class=\"big\">{{ total.current }}</span>\n          <span class=\"unit\">events this week</span>\n        </div>\n\n        <div class=\"verdict\">\n          @if (hasBadge(total.status)) {\n            <span class=\"badge\" [class]=\"'badge-' + total.status\">{{ statusLabel(total.status) }}</span>\n          } @else {\n            <span class=\"muted-verdict\">{{ statusLabel(total.status) }}</span>\n          }\n          <span class=\"delta\" [class]=\"deltaClass(total)\">{{ deltaText(total) }}</span>\n        </div>\n\n        <dl class=\"context\">\n          <div>\n            <dt>Typical for you</dt>\n            <dd>{{ total.baselineMedian | number:'1.0-1' }}</dd>\n          </div>\n          <div>\n            <dt>Normal range</dt>\n            <dd>{{ total.typicalLow | number:'1.0-0' }} – {{ total.typicalHigh | number:'1.0-0' }}</dd>\n          </div>\n          <div>\n            <dt>Baseline</dt>\n            <dd>{{ total.baselineWeeksUsed }} weeks to {{ formatDate(p.baseline.end) }}</dd>\n          </div>\n        </dl>\n      </div>\n\n      <p class=\"footnote\">\n        “Typical” is the median of your own previous {{ total.baselineWeeksUsed }} weeks, so a\n        one-off spike does not redefine normal.\n        @if (p.dataQuality.duplicateEventsExcluded > 0) {\n          {{ p.dataQuality.duplicateEventsExcluded }} duplicate event{{ p.dataQuality.duplicateEventsExcluded === 1 ? '' : 's' }} excluded.\n        }\n      </p>\n    </section>\n\n    <section class=\"card\">\n      <h3>Sites, most unusual first</h3>\n\n      @if (p.locations.length === 0) {\n        <p class=\"muted\">No site recorded activity in this window.</p>\n      } @else {\n        <table>\n          <thead>\n            <tr>\n              <th scope=\"col\">Site</th>\n              <th scope=\"col\" class=\"num\">This week</th>\n              <th scope=\"col\" class=\"num\">Typical</th>\n              <th scope=\"col\" class=\"num\">Normal range</th>\n              <th scope=\"col\" class=\"num\">Change</th>\n              <th scope=\"col\">Status</th>\n            </tr>\n          </thead>\n          <tbody>\n            @for (row of p.locations; track row.location) {\n              <tr [class.attention]=\"row.metric.status === 'below' || row.metric.status === 'above'\">\n                <th scope=\"row\">{{ row.location }}</th>\n                <td class=\"num strong\">{{ row.metric.current }}</td>\n                <td class=\"num\">{{ row.metric.baselineMedian | number:'1.0-1' }}</td>\n                <td class=\"num range\">\n                  {{ row.metric.typicalLow | number:'1.0-0' }} – {{ row.metric.typicalHigh | number:'1.0-0' }}\n                </td>\n                <td class=\"num\" [class]=\"deltaClass(row.metric)\">{{ deltaText(row.metric) }}</td>\n                <td>\n                  @if (hasBadge(row.metric.status)) {\n                    <span class=\"badge\" [class]=\"'badge-' + row.metric.status\">{{ statusLabel(row.metric.status) }}</span>\n                  } @else {\n                    <!-- Degraded states stay visible and unstyled-as-a-verdict on purpose. -->\n                    <span class=\"muted-verdict\">{{ statusLabel(row.metric.status) }}</span>\n                  }\n                </td>\n              </tr>\n            }\n          </tbody>\n        </table>\n      }\n    </section>\n  }\n}\n"

It does because it lives\n * nowhere but the URL — so what is worth testing is that a component built fresh from a\n * URL (which is exactly what a reload produces) reconstructs the same state and asks the\n * API for the same thing, with nothing carried over in memory.\n */\ndescribe('PulsePageComponent — URL-persisted filter state', () => {\n  const FULL_URL = '/?accountId=6&baselineWeeks=12&eventType=call_received&weekStart=2026-07-20';\n\n  let http: HttpTestingController;\n\n  /** Answers whatever the component has asked for so far. */\n  const settle = () =>\n    http.match(() => true).forEach((r) =>\n      r.flush(r.request.url.endsWith('/api/accounts') ? [] : PULSE));\n\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    TestBed.configureTestingModule({\n      providers: [\n        provideRouter([{ path: '', component: PulsePageComponent }]),\n        provideHttpClient(),\n        provideHttpClientTesting(),\n      ],\n    });\n    http = TestBed.inject(HttpTestingController);\n  });\n\n  afterEach(() => http.verify());\n\n  it('rebuilds every filter from the URL alone, as a reload would', async () => {\n    const harness = await RouterTestingHarness.create(FULL_URL);\n    const component = harness.routeDebugElement!.componentInstance as PulsePageComponent;\n\n    expect(component.filters()).toEqual({\n      accountId: 6,\n      baselineWeeks: 12,\n      eventType: 'call_received',\n      weekStart: '2026-07-20',\n    });\n\n    settle();\n  });\n\n  it('asks the API for exactly what the URL said, holding nothing in memory', async () => {\n    await RouterTestingHarness.create(FULL_URL);\n\n    http.expectOne('/api/accounts').flush([]);\n\n    const request = http.expectOne((r) => r.url === '/api/accounts/6/weekly-pulse');\n    expect(request.request.params.get('baselineWeeks')).toBe('12');\n    expect(request.request.params.get('eventType')).toBe('call_received');\n    expect(request.request.params.get('weekStart')).toBe('2026-07-20');\n    request.flush(PULSE);\n  });\n\n  it('writes a changed filter back into the URL so the next load keeps it', async () => {\n    const harness = await RouterTestingHarness.create('/?accountId=6');\n    const component = harness.routeDebugElement!.componentInstance as PulsePageComponent;\n    settle();\n\n    await component.patch({ baselineWeeks: 12 });\n\n    const url = TestBed.inject(Router).url;\n    expect(url).toContain('baselineWeeks=12');\n    expect(url).toContain('accountId=6');   // merged into the URL, not replacing it\n\n    settle();\n  });\n\n  it('drops the week when the account changes, so one account cannot pin another', async () => {\n    const harness = await RouterTestingHarness.create('/?accountId=6&weekStart=2026-07-20');\n    const component = harness.routeDebugElement!.componentInstance as PulsePageComponent;\n    settle();\n\n    await component.patch({ accountId: 16, weekStart: null });\n\n    expect(TestBed.inject(Router).url).not.toContain('weekStart');\n    expect(component.filters().weekStart).toBeNull();\n\n    settle();\n  });\n});\n\n/**\n * A URL is user-editable and can be stale — a bookmark saved before an option changed, or\n * a hand-typed value. ## ASSISTANT  <sub>08/14/2026 01:47:39</sub>

Se ve bien, **pero hay un bug real**: los tres `<select>` muestran la primera opción, no la seleccionada. La cuenta dice "Beacon Home Security" mientras los datos son "Metro Collision Centers", y "Compare against" dice 4 semanas cuando el baseline es de 8.

Causa: `[value]` en un `<select>` se aplica antes de que `@for` renderice las opciones, así que se pierde. Se arregla marcando la opción, no el select:
